=== 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd') {\n
this.paddle.vx = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  handleLaunch() {\n    if (this.state === 
'ready' || (this.state === 'playing' && this.balls.some(b => b.stuck))) {\n     
const stuckBall = this.balls.find(b => b.stuck);\n      if (stuckBall) {\n      
stuckBall.stuck = false;\n        stuckBall.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;\n 
stuckBall.vy = -300;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  togglePause() {\n    if 
(this.state === 'playing') {\n      this.state = 'paused';\n      this.isPaused 
= true;\n    } else if (this.state === 'paused') {\n      this.state = 
'playing';\n      this.isPaused = false;\n      this.accumulator = 0;\n    }\n  
}\n\n  handleRestart() {\n    if (this.state === 'game-over' && this.gameState) 
{\n      window.ui.startNewRun(this.currentLevel.level_number || 
this.currentLevel.level);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async loadLevel(levelNumber) {\n    
const gameState = await window.api.getGameState();\n    \n    const levels = 
gameState.user;\n    const levelData = {\n      level_number: levelNumber,\n    
base_speed: gameState.activeRun ? gameState.activeRun.level : levelNumber,\n    
speed_cap: 520 + (levelNumber - 1) * 25\n    };\n\n    this.currentLevel = 
levelData;\n    this.currentBrickLayout = gameState.activeRun?.bricks || {};\n  
\n    this.gameState = gameState.activeRun || {\n      run_id: null,\n      
revision: 0,\n      level: levelNumber,\n      score: 0,\n      lives: 3,\n     
combo: 1,\n      next_extra_life: 20000,\n      power_up: null,\n      
power_seconds: 0,\n      paddle_width: 118,\n      balls: [{ x: 189, y: 540, vx:
0, vy: 0, stuck: true, primary: true }],\n      drops: [],\n      bricks: 
this.currentBrickLayout,\n      accumulated_time: 0\n    };\n\n    this.score = 
this.gameState.score;\n    this.lives = this.gameState.lives;\n    this.combo = 
this.gameState.combo;\n    this.nextExtraLife = 
this.gameState.next_extra_life;\n    this.powerUp = this.gameState.power_up;\n  
this.powerSeconds = this.gameState.power_seconds || 0;\n    this.paddle.width = 
this.gameState.paddle_width;\n    this.balls = this.gameState.balls.map(b => ({ 
...b }));\n    this.drops = this.gameState.drops.map(d => ({ ...d }));\n    
this.bricks = this.gameState.bricks ? 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.gameState.bricks)) : {};\n    \n    
this.accumulator = this.gameState.accumulated_time || 0;\n    \n    if 
(!this.gameState.run_id) {\n      const response = await 
window.api.startRun(levelNumber);\n      this.gameState = response;\n      
this.gameState.balls = this.balls;\n      this.gameState.drops = this.drops;\n  
this.gameState.bricks = this.bricks;\n    }\n\n    this.state = 'ready';\n    
this.startGameLoop();\n  }\n\n  startGameLoop() {\n    const animate = (time) =>
{\n      if (!this.lastFrameTime) {\n        this.lastFrameTime = time;\n      
}\n\n      const deltaTime = Math.min((time - this.lastFrameTime) / 1000, 
0.033);\n      this.lastFrameTime = time;\n\n      if (!this.isPaused && 
this.state === 'playing') {\n        this.accumulator += deltaTime;\n        
this.accumulator = Math.min(this.accumulator, this.MAX_ACCUMULATOR);\n\n        
while (this.accumulator >= this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP) {\n          
this.update(this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP);\n          this.accumulator -= 
this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP;\n        }\n      }\n\n      this.render();\n      
this.animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n    };\n\n    
this.animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n  }\n\n  update(dt) {\n
if (this.state !== 'playing') return;\n\n    this.updatePaddle(dt);\n    
this.updateBalls(dt);\n    this.updateDrops(dt);\n    
this.updatePowerUp(dt);\n\n    const allBroken = this.checkLevelComplete();\n   
if (allBroken) {\n      this.completeLevel();\n    }\n\n    const noLives = 
this.checkGameOver();\n    if (noLives) {\n      this.endGame();\n    }\n  }\n\n
updatePaddle(dt) {\n    this.paddle.x += this.paddle.vx * dt;\n    this.paddle.x
= Math.max(\n      this.paddle.width / 2,\n      Math.min(this.canvasWidth - 
this.paddle.width / 2, this.paddle.x)\n    );\n    this.lastPaddleX = 
this.paddle.x;\n  }\n\n  updateBalls(dt) {\n    for (const ball of this.balls) 
{\n      if (ball.stuck) {\n        ball.x = this.paddle.x;\n        ball.y = 
this.paddle.y - 12;\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      const startX = 
ball.x;\n      const startY = ball.y;\n\n      ball.x += ball.vx * dt;\n      
ball.y += ball.vy * dt;\n\n      this.ensureMinimumSpeed(ball);\n      
this.capBallSpeed(ball);\n\n      this.checkWallCollisions(ball, startX, startY,
dt);\n      this.checkPaddleCollision(ball, startX, startY);\n      
this.checkBrickCollisions(ball, startX, startY);\n    }\n  }\n\n  
ensureMinimumSpeed(ball) {\n    const minSpeed = 100;\n    const speed = 
Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);\n    if (speed < minSpeed && speed > 0) {\n      
const scale = minSpeed / speed;\n      ball.vx *= scale;\n      ball.vy *= 
scale;\n    }\n  }\n\n  capBallSpeed(ball) {\n    const levelSpeed = 
this.currentLevel?.speed_cap || 520;\n    const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, 
ball.vy);\n    if (speed > levelSpeed) {\n      const scale = levelSpeed / 
speed;\n      ball.vx *= scale;\n      ball.vy *= scale;\n    }\n  }\n\n  
checkWallCollisions(ball, startX, startY, dt) {\n    const radius = 6;\n\n    if
(ball.x - radius < 0) {\n      ball.x = radius;\n      ball.vx = 
Math.abs(ball.vx);\n    } else if (ball.x + radius > this.canvasWidth) {\n      
ball.x = this.canvasWidth - radius;\n      ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx);\n    
}\n\n    if (ball.y - radius < 0) {\n      ball.y = radius;\n      ball.vy = 
Math.abs(ball.vy);\n    } else if (ball.y > this.canvasHeight) {\n      
this.loseBall(ball);\n    }\n  }\n\n  checkPaddleCollision(ball, startX, startY)
{\n    const paddleTop = this.paddle.y - this.paddle.height / 2;\n    const 
paddleBottom = this.paddle.y + this.paddle.height / 2;\n    const paddleLeft = 
this.paddle.x - this.paddle.width / 2;\n    const paddleRight = this.paddle.x + 
this.paddle.width / 2;\n    const ballRadius = 6;\n\n    if (\n      ball.x + 
ballRadius >= paddleLeft &&\n      ball.x - ballRadius <= paddleRight &&\n      
ball.y + ballRadius >= paddleTop &&\n      ball.y - ballRadius <= paddleBottom 
&&\n      startY < paddleTop\n    ) {\n      ball.y = paddleTop - ballRadius;\n 
\n      const contactPos = (ball.x - paddleLeft) / this.paddle.width;\n      
const angle = (contactPos - 0.5) * Math.PI * 0.4;\n      const speed = 
Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) || 300;\n      \n      ball.vx = Math.sin(angle) * 
speed * 0.8 + this.paddle.vx * 0.3;\n      ball.vy = -Math.cos(angle) * speed;\n
\n      this.combo = 1;\n      \n      if (this.powerUp === 'sticky' && 
!ball.stuck) {\n        ball.stuck = true;\n        this.powerUp = null;\n      
this.powerSeconds = 0;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  checkBrickCollisions(ball, 
startX, startY) {\n    const ballRadius = 6;\n    const gridSize = 30;\n\n    
for (const key in this.bricks) {\n      const brick = this.bricks[key];\n      
if (brick.health <= 0) continue;\n\n      const [row, col] = 
key.split(',').map(Number);\n      const brickX = col * gridSize + gridSize / 
2;\n      const brickY = row * gridSize + gridSize / 2;\n      const brickW = 
gridSize - 2;\n      const brickH = gridSize - 2;\n\n      if 
(this.sweptCollision(startX, startY, ball.x, ball.y, ballRadius, brickX, brickY,
brickW, brickH)) {\n        const damage = this.damageBrick(key, brick);\n      
if (damage > 0) {\n          this.addScore(damage);\n          \n          const
closestPoint = this.closestPointOnRect(ball.x, ball.y, brickX - brickW/2, brickY
- brickH/2, brickW, brickH);\n          const dx = ball.x - closestPoint.x;\n   
const dy = ball.y - closestPoint.y;\n          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, 
dy);\n          \n          if (dist > 0) {\n            ball.vx = (dx / dist) *
Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 0.8;\n            ball.vy = (dy / dist) * 
Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 0.8;\n          }\n          \n          if 
(brick.drop && Math.random() < 0.3) {\n            this.spawnDrop(brickX, 
brickY, brick.drop);\n          }\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n  
}\n\n  sweptCollision(x1, y1, x2, y2, r, bx, by, bw, bh) {\n    const left = bx 
- bw / 2;\n    const right = bx + bw / 2;\n    const top = by - bh / 2;\n    
const bottom = by + bh / 2;\n\n    const dx = x2 - x1;\n    const dy = y2 - 
y1;\n    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n\n    if (dist === 0) {\n      return
x2 > left && x2 < right && y2 > top && y2 < bottom;\n    }\n\n    const t = 
Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((bx - x1) * dx + (by - y1) * dy) / (dist * dist)));\n  
const closestX = x1 + t * dx;\n    const closestY = y1 + t * dy;\n\n    const 
distToBrick = Math.hypot(bx - closestX, by - closestY);\n    return distToBrick 
<= r;\n  }\n\n  closestPointOnRect(px, py, left, top, width, height) {\n    
return {\n      x: Math.max(left, Math.min(px, left + width)),\n      y: 
Math.max(top, Math.min(py, top + height))\n    };\n  }\n\n  damageBrick(key, 
brick) {\n    if (brick.type === 'solid') {\n      return 0;\n    }\n    \n    
if (brick.health <= 0) {\n      return 0;\n    }\n\n    brick.health--;\n    \n 
if (brick.type === 'normal') {\n      if (brick.health === 0) {\n        return 
100 * this.combo;\n      }\n    } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {\n      if 
(brick.health === 1) {\n        return 75 * this.combo;\n      } else if 
(brick.health === 0) {\n        return 250 * this.combo;\n      }\n    }\n    \n
return 0;\n  }\n\n  addScore(points) {\n    const oldScore = this.score;\n    
this.score += points;\n    \n    const oldThreshold = Math.floor(oldScore / 
20000);\n    const newThreshold = Math.floor(this.score / 20000);\n    \n    if 
(newThreshold > oldThreshold) {\n      this.lives++;\n      this.nextExtraLife =
(newThreshold + 1) * 20000;\n    }\n    \n    this.combo = Math.min(5, 
this.combo + 1);\n  }\n\n  checkLevelComplete() {\n    return 
Object.values(this.bricks).every(b => b.type === 'solid' || b.health <= 0);\n  
}\n\n  completeLevel() {\n    this.state = 'level-complete';\n    const bonus = 
this.currentLevel.level_number * 1000;\n    this.score += bonus;\n    
this.gameState.outcome = 'level-complete';\n    this.gameState.next_level = 
this.currentLevel.level_number + 1;\n  }\n\n  checkGameOver() {\n    return 
this.lives === 0;\n  }\n\n  endGame() {\n    this.state = 'game-over';\n  }\n\n 
loseBall(ball) {\n    const idx = this.balls.indexOf(ball);\n    if (idx !== -1)
{\n      this.balls.splice(idx, 1);\n    }\n\n    if (this.balls.length === 0) 
{\n      this.lives--;\n      if (this.lives <= 0) {\n        this.endGame();\n 
} else {\n        this.state = 'life-lost';\n        this.powerUp = null;\n     
this.powerSeconds = 0;\n        this.drops = [];\n        setTimeout(() => {\n  
if (this.state === 'life-lost') {\n            this.balls = [{\n              x:
this.paddle.x,\n              y: this.paddle.y - 12,\n              vx: 0,\n    
vy: 0,\n              stuck: true,\n              primary: true\n            
}];\n            this.state = 'ready';\n          }\n        }, 1000);\n      
}\n    }\n  }\n\n  updateDrops(dt) {\n    for (let i = this.drops.length - 1; i 
>= 0; i--) {\n      const drop = this.drops[i];\n      drop.y += 200 * dt;\n\n  
const paddleTop = this.paddle.y - this.paddle.height / 2;\n      const 
paddleBottom = this.paddle.y + this.paddle.height / 2;\n      const paddleLeft =
this.paddle.x - this.paddle.width / 2;\n      const paddleRight = this.paddle.x 
+ this.paddle.width / 2;\n\n      if (\n        drop.x >= paddleLeft &&\n       
drop.x <= paddleRight &&\n        drop.y >= paddleTop &&\n        drop.y <= 
paddleBottom\n      ) {\n        this.collectPowerUp(drop.type);\n        
this.drops.splice(i, 1);\n      } else if (drop.y > this.canvasHeight) {\n      
this.drops.splice(i, 1);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  spawnDrop(x, y, type) {\n    
this.drops.push({\n      x,\n      y,\n      type,\n      vx: 0,\n      vy: 0\n 
});\n  }\n\n  collectPowerUp(type) {\n    if (this.powerUp && this.powerUp !== 
type) {\n      this.removePowerUp();\n    }\n\n    this.powerUp = type;\n    
this.powerSeconds = 20;\n\n    if (type === 'wide') {\n      this.paddle.width =
this.paddle.baseWidth * 1.5;\n    } else if (type === 'slow') {\n      for 
(const ball of this.balls) {\n        ball.vx *= 0.7;\n        ball.vy *= 0.7;\n
}\n    } else if (type === 'multiball') {\n      if (this.balls.length === 1) 
{\n        const primaryBall = this.balls[0];\n        this.balls.push({\n      
x: primaryBall.x - 20,\n          y: primaryBall.y,\n          vx: 
primaryBall.vx - 100,\n          vy: primaryBall.vy,\n          stuck: false,\n 
primary: false\n        });\n      }\n    } else if (type === 'sticky') {\n     
// No immediate action\n    }\n  }\n\n  removePowerUp() {\n    if (this.powerUp 
=== 'wide') {\n      this.paddle.width = this.paddle.baseWidth;\n    } else if 
(this.powerUp === 'slow') {\n      for (const ball of this.balls) {\n        
const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);\n        const newSpeed = 
Math.min(speed / 0.7, this.currentLevel?.speed_cap || 520);\n        if (speed >
0) {\n          ball.vx = (ball.vx / speed) * newSpeed;\n          ball.vy = 
(ball.vy / speed) * newSpeed;\n        }\n      }\n    } else if (this.powerUp 
=== 'multiball') {\n      this.balls = this.balls.filter(b => b.primary !== 
false || !b.secondary_only);\n    }\n  }\n\n  updatePowerUp(dt) {\n    if 
(this.powerUp && this.powerSeconds > 0) {\n      this.powerSeconds -= dt;\n     
if (this.powerSeconds <= 0) {\n        this.removePowerUp();\n        
this.powerUp = null;\n        this.powerSeconds = 0;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  
render() {\n    this.ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0f1e';\n    this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 
this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);\n\n    this.renderBricks();\n    
this.renderDrops();\n    this.renderPaddle();\n    this.renderBalls();\n    
this.renderUI();\n  }\n\n  renderBricks() {\n    const gridSize = 30;\n    const
colors = {\n      normal: '#56c7ff',\n      strong: '#ffd166',\n      solid: 
'#6b6b7e'\n    };\n\n    for (const key in this.bricks) {\n      const brick = 
this.bricks[key];\n      const [row, col] = key.split(',').map(Number);\n      
const x = col * gridSize + 1;\n      const y = row * gridSize + 1;\n\n      if 
(brick.health <= 0 && brick.type !== 'solid') continue;\n\n      
this.ctx.fillStyle = colors[brick.type] || '#56c7ff';\n      
this.ctx.fillRect(x, y, gridSize - 2, gridSize - 2);\n\n      if (brick.type ===
'strong' && brick.health > 0) {\n        this.ctx.strokeStyle = brick.health ===
1 ? '#ff6b6b' : '#ffd166';\n        this.ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n        
this.ctx.strokeRect(x, y, gridSize - 2, gridSize - 2);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n 
renderDrops() {\n    for (const drop of this.drops) {\n      const colors = {\n 
wide: '#56c7ff',\n        slow: '#7ee787',\n        multiball: '#ffd166',\n     
sticky: '#ff6b6b'\n      };\n      \n      this.ctx.fillStyle = 
colors[drop.type] || '#56c7ff';\n      this.ctx.beginPath();\n      
this.ctx.arc(drop.x, drop.y, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      this.ctx.fill();\n    
}\n  }\n\n  renderPaddle() {\n    this.ctx.fillStyle = '#7ee787';\n    
this.ctx.fillRect(\n      this.paddle.x - this.paddle.width / 2,\n      
this.paddle.y - this.paddle.height / 2,\n      this.paddle.width,\n      
this.paddle.height\n    );\n  }\n\n  renderBalls() {\n    for (const ball of 
this.balls) {\n      this.ctx.fillStyle = ball.primary ? '#56c7ff' : 
'#ffd166';\n      this.ctx.beginPath();\n      this.ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, 6, 
0, Math.PI * 2);\n      this.ctx.fill();\n    }\n  }\n\n  renderUI() {\n    if 
(this.state === 'ready') {\n      this.ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)';\n  
this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);\n      \n      
this.ctx.fillStyle = '#56c7ff';\n      this.ctx.font = 'bold 24px sans-serif';\n
this.ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n      this.ctx.fillText('Press Space to Launch',
this.canvasWidth / 2, this.canvasHeight / 2);\n    } else if (this.state === 
'paused') {\n      this.ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6)';\n      
this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);\n      \n      
this.ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd166';\n      this.ctx.font = 'bold 24px sans-serif';\n
this.ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n      this.ctx.fillText('PAUSED', 
this.canvasWidth / 2, this.canvasHeight / 2);\n    } else if (this.state === 
'life-lost') {\n      this.ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)';\n      
this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);\n      \n      
this.ctx.fillStyle = '#ff6b6b';\n      this.ctx.font = 'bold 24px sans-serif';\n
this.ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n      this.ctx.fillText('LIFE LOST', 
this.canvasWidth / 2, this.canvasHeight / 2);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async saveState()
{\n    if (!this.gameState || !this.gameState.run_id) return;\n\n    try {\n    
const stateToSave = {\n        level: this.currentLevel.level_number,\n        
score: this.score,\n        lives: this.lives,\n        combo: this.combo,\n    
next_extra_life: this.nextExtraLife,\n        power_up: this.powerUp,\n        
power_seconds: this.powerSeconds,\n        paddle_width: this.paddle.width,\n   
balls: this.balls,\n        drops: this.drops,\n        bricks: this.bricks,\n  
accumulated_time: this.accumulator\n      };\n\n      await 
window.api.saveRun(\n        this.gameState.run_id,\n        
this.gameState.revision,\n        stateToSave\n      );\n\n      
this.gameState.revision++;\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error('Error 
saving state:', err);\n    }\n  }\n\n  destroy() {\n    if 
(this.animationFrameId) {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(this.animationFrameId);\n 
}\n  }\n}\n\nwindow.game = new BrickfallGame();\n"}

Reasoning:
Now let me create the game engine. This is the most complex part with the 
physics, collision detection, and game logic. I need to implement:

1. Game state management (menu, ready, playing, paused, life-lost, 
level-complete, game-over, completed)
2. Physics with fixed timestep (1/120 second)
3. Ball physics with swept collision
4. Paddle control with assist mode
5. Brick collision and damage
6. Power-ups (wide, slow, multiball, sticky)
7. Scoring and combo system
8. Extra lives at 20k thresholds
9. Level progression
10. Save/load functionality
11. Proper accumulator for bounded time stepping

Let me create a comprehensive game.js file.

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/js/game.js"
  file_text: 
    class BrickfallGame {
      constructor() {
        this.canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
        this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
        
        this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP = 1 / 120;
        this.MAX_ACCUMULATOR = this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP * 10;
        this.accumulator = 0;
        
        this.canvasWidth = 378;
        this.canvasHeight = 600;
        
        this.state = 'menu';
        this.gameState = null;
        this.currentLevel = null;
        this.currentBrickLayout = null;
        
        this.paddle = {
          x: 189,
          y: 560,
          width: 118,
          height: 16,
          baseWidth: 118,
          speed: 400,
          vx: 0
        };
        
        this.balls = [];
        this.drops = [];
        this.bricks = {};
        
        this.score = 0;
        this.lives = 3;
        this.combo = 1;
        this.nextExtraLife = 20000;
        
        this.powerUp = null;
        this.powerSeconds = 0;
        
        this.lastPaddleX = this.paddle.x;
        this.isPaused = false;
        this.assistPaddle = false;
        
        this.animationFrameId = null;
        this.lastFrameTime = 0;
        
        this.setupEventListeners();
      }
    
      setupEventListeners() {
        document.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => 
this.handlePointerMove(e));
        document.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => this.handleTouchMove(e));
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => this.handleKeyDown(e));
        document.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => this.handleKeyUp(e));
        
        document.getElementById('assist-paddle').addEventListener('change', (e) 
=> {
          this.assistPaddle = e.target.checked;
          if (this.assistPaddle === false) {
            this.assistPaddle = false;
          }
        });
      }
    
      handlePointerMove(e) {
        if (this.assistPaddle === false && e.pointerType !== 'touch') {
          this.updatePaddleFromPointer(e.clientX);
        }
      }
    
      handleTouchMove(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const touch = e.touches[0];
        this.updatePaddleFromPointer(touch.clientX);
        this.assistPaddle = false;
      }
    
      updatePaddleFromPointer(clientX) {
        const rect = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        const relativeX = clientX - rect.left;
        const canvasX = (relativeX / rect.width) * this.canvasWidth;
        
        this.paddle.x = Math.max(
          this.paddle.width / 2,
          Math.min(this.canvasWidth - this.paddle.width / 2, canvasX)
        );
        this.paddle.vx = (this.paddle.x - this.lastPaddleX) / (1/60);
        this.lastPaddleX = this.paddle.x;
      }
    
      handleKeyDown(e) {
        if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a') {
          this.paddle.vx = -this.paddle.speed;
          this.assistPaddle = false;
        } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd') {
          this.paddle.vx = this.paddle.speed;
          this.assistPaddle = false;
        } else if (e.key === ' ') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.handleLaunch();
        } else if (e.key === 'p' || e.key === 'P' || e.key === 'Escape') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.togglePause();
        } else if (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (this.state === 'game-over') {
            this.handleRestart();
          }
        }
      }
    
      handleKeyUp(e) {
        if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'ArrowRight' || 
e.key === 'd') {
          this.paddle.vx = 0;
        }
      }
    
      handleLaunch() {
        if (this.state === 'ready' || (this.state === 'playing' && 
this.balls.some(b => b.stuck))) {
          const stuckBall = this.balls.find(b => b.stuck);
          if (stuckBall) {
            stuckBall.stuck = false;
            stuckBall.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
            stuckBall.vy = -300;
          }
        }
      }
    
      togglePause() {
        if (this.state === 'playing') {
          this.state = 'paused';
          this.isPaused = true;
        } else if (this.state === 'paused') {
          this.state = 'playing';
          this.isPaused = false;
          this.accumulator = 0;
        }
      }
    
      handleRestart() {
        if (this.state === 'game-over' && this.gameState) {
          window.ui.startNewRun(this.currentLevel.level_number || 
this.currentLevel.level);
        }
      }
    
      async loadLevel(levelNumber) {
        const gameState = await window.api.getGameState();
        
        const levels = gameState.user;
        const levelData = {
          level_number: levelNumber,
          base_speed: gameState.activeRun ? gameState.activeRun.level : 
levelNumber,
          speed_cap: 520 + (levelNumber - 1) * 25
        };
    
        this.currentLevel = levelData;
        this.currentBrickLayout = gameState.activeRun?.bricks || {};
        
        this.gameState = gameState.activeRun || {
          run_id: null,
          revision: 0,
          level: levelNumber,
          score: 0,
          lives: 3,
          combo: 1,
          next_extra_life: 20000,
          power_up: null,
          power_seconds: 0,
          paddle_width: 118,
          balls: [{ x: 189, y: 540, vx: 0, vy: 0, stuck: true, primary: true }],
          drops: [],
          bricks: this.currentBrickLayout,
          accumulated_time: 0
        };
    
        this.score = this.gameState.score;
        this.lives = this.gameState.lives;
        this.combo = this.gameState.combo;
        this.nextExtraLife = this.gameState.next_extra_life;
        this.powerUp = this.gameState.power_up;
        this.powerSeconds = this.gameState.power_seconds || 0;
        this.paddle.width = this.gameState.paddle_width;
        this.balls = this.gameState.balls.map(b => ({ ...b }));
        this.drops = this.gameState.drops.map(d => ({ ...d }));
        this.bricks = this.gameState.bricks ? 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.gameState.bricks)) : {};
        
        this.accumulator = this.gameState.accumulated_time || 0;
        
        if (!this.gameState.run_id) {
          const response = await window.api.startRun(levelNumber);
          this.gameState = response;
          this.gameState.balls = this.balls;
          this.gameState.drops = this.drops;
          this.gameState.bricks = this.bricks;
        }
    
        this.state = 'ready';
        this.startGameLoop();
      }
    
      startGameLoop() {
        const animate = (time) => {
          if (!this.lastFrameTime) {
            this.lastFrameTime = time;
          }
    
          const deltaTime = Math.min((time - this.lastFrameTime) / 1000, 0.033);
          this.lastFrameTime = time;
    
          if (!this.isPaused && this.state === 'playing') {
            this.accumulator += deltaTime;
            this.accumulator = Math.min(this.accumulator, this.MAX_ACCUMULATOR);
    
            while (this.accumulator >= this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP) {
              this.update(this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP);
              this.accumulator -= this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP;
            }
          }
    
          this.render();
          this.animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        };
    
        this.animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      }
    
      update(dt) {
        if (this.state !== 'playing') return;
    
        this.updatePaddle(dt);
        this.updateBalls(dt);
        this.updateDrops(dt);
        this.updatePowerUp(dt);
    
        const allBroken = this.checkLevelComplete();
        if (allBroken) {
          this.completeLevel();
        }
    
        const noLives = this.checkGameOver();
        if (noLives) {
          this.endGame();
        }
      }
    
      updatePaddle(dt) {
        this.paddle.x += this.paddle.vx * dt;
        this.paddle.x = Math.max(
          this.paddle.width / 2,
          Math.min(this.canvasWidth - this.paddle.width / 2, this.paddle.x)
        );
        this.lastPaddleX = this.paddle.x;
      }
    
      updateBalls(dt) {
        for (const ball of this.balls) {
          if (ball.stuck) {
            ball.x = this.paddle.x;
            ball.y = this.paddle.y - 12;
            continue;
          }
    
          const startX = ball.x;
          const startY = ball.y;
    
          ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
          ball.y += ball.vy * dt;
    
          this.ensureMinimumSpeed(ball);
          this.capBallSpeed(ball);
    
          this.checkWallCollisions(ball, startX, startY, dt);
          this.checkPaddleCollision(ball, startX, startY);
          this.checkBrickCollisions(ball, startX, startY);
        }
      }
    
      ensureMinimumSpeed(ball) {
        const minSpeed = 100;
        const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
        if (speed < minSpeed && speed > 0) {
          const scale = minSpeed / speed;
          ball.vx *= scale;
          ball.vy *= scale;
        }
      }
    
      capBallSpeed(ball) {
        const levelSpeed = this.currentLevel?.speed_cap || 520;
        const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
        if (speed > levelSpeed) {
          const scale = levelSpeed / speed;
          ball.vx *= scale;
          ball.vy *= scale;
        }
      }
    
      checkWallCollisions(ball, startX, startY, dt) {
        const radius = 6;
    
        if (ball.x - radius < 0) {
          ball.x = radius;
          ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx);
        } else if (ball.x + radius > this.canvasWidth) {
          ball.x = this.canvasWidth - radius;
          ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx);
        }
    
        if (ball.y - radius < 0) {
          ball.y = radius;
          ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy);
        } else if (ball.y > this.canvasHeight) {
          this.loseBall(ball);
        }
      }
    
      checkPaddleCollision(ball, startX, startY) {
        const paddleTop = this.paddle.y - this.paddle.height / 2;
        const paddleBottom = this.paddle.y + this.paddle.height / 2;
        const paddleLeft = this.paddle.x - this.paddle.width / 2;
        const paddleRight = this.paddle.x + this.paddle.width / 2;
        const ballRadius = 6;
    
        if (
          ball.x + ballRadius >= paddleLeft &&
          ball.x - ballRadius <= paddleRight &&
          ball.y + ballRadius >= paddleTop &&
          ball.y - ballRadius <= paddleBottom &&
          startY < paddleTop
        ) {
          ball.y = paddleTop - ballRadius;
          
          const contactPos = (ball.x - paddleLeft) / this.paddle.width;
          const angle = (contactPos - 0.5) * Math.PI * 0.4;
          const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) || 300;
          
          ball.vx = Math.sin(angle) * speed * 0.8 + this.paddle.vx * 0.3;
          ball.vy = -Math.cos(angle) * speed;
          
          this.combo = 1;
          
          if (this.powerUp === 'sticky' && !ball.stuck) {
            ball.stuck = true;
            this.powerUp = null;
            this.powerSeconds = 0;
          }
        }
      }
    
      checkBrickCollisions(ball, startX, startY) {
        const ballRadius = 6;
        const gridSize = 30;
    
        for (const key in this.bricks) {
          const brick = this.bricks[key];
          if (brick.health <= 0) continue;
    
          const [row, col] = key.split(',').map(Number);
          const brickX = col * gridSize + gridSize / 2;
          const brickY = row * gridSize + gridSize / 2;
          const brickW = gridSize - 2;
          const brickH = gridSize - 2;
    
          if (this.sweptCollision(startX, startY, ball.x, ball.y, ballRadius, 
brickX, brickY, brickW, brickH)) {
            const damage = this.damageBrick(key, brick);
            if (damage > 0) {
              this.addScore(damage);
              
              const closestPoint = this.closestPointOnRect(ball.x, ball.y, 
brickX - brickW/2, brickY - brickH/2, brickW, brickH);
              const dx = ball.x - closestPoint.x;
              const dy = ball.y - closestPoint.y;
              const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
              
              if (dist > 0) {
                ball.vx = (dx / dist) * Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 0.8;
                ball.vy = (dy / dist) * Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 0.8;
              }
              
              if (brick.drop && Math.random() < 0.3) {
                this.spawnDrop(brickX, brickY, brick.drop);
              }
            }
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    
      sweptCollision(x1, y1, x2, y2, r, bx, by, bw, bh) {
        const left = bx - bw / 2;
        const right = bx + bw / 2;
        const top = by - bh / 2;
        const bottom = by + bh / 2;
    
        const dx = x2 - x1;
        const dy = y2 - y1;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    
        if (dist === 0) {
          return x2 > left && x2 < right && y2 > top && y2 < bottom;
        }
    
        const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((bx - x1) * dx + (by - y1) * dy) / 
(dist * dist)));
        const closestX = x1 + t * dx;
        const closestY = y1 + t * dy;
    
        const distToBrick = Math.hypot(bx - closestX, by - closestY);
        return distToBrick <= r;
      }
    
      closestPointOnRect(px, py, left, top, width, height) {
        return {
          x: Math.max(left, Math.min(px, left + width)),
          y: Math.max(top, Math.min(py, top + height))
        };
      }
    
      damageBrick(key, brick) {
        if (brick.type === 'solid') {
          return 0;
        }
        
        if (brick.health <= 0) {
          return 0;
        }
    
        brick.health--;
        
        if (brick.type === 'normal') {
          if (brick.health === 0) {
            return 100 * this.combo;
          }
        } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
          if (brick.health === 1) {
            return 75 * this.combo;
          } else if (brick.health === 0) {
            return 250 * this.combo;
          }
        }
        
        return 0;
      }
    
      addScore(points) {
        const oldScore = this.score;
        this.score += points;
        
        const oldThreshold = Math.floor(oldScore / 20000);
        const newThreshold = Math.floor(this.score / 20000);
        
        if (newThreshold > oldThreshold) {
          this.lives++;
          this.nextExtraLife = (newThreshold + 1) * 20000;
        }
        
        this.combo = Math.min(5, this.combo + 1);
      }
    
      checkLevelComplete() {
        return Object.values(this.bricks).every(b => b.type === 'solid' || 
b.health <= 0);
      }
    
      completeLevel() {
        this.state = 'level-complete';
        const bonus = this.currentLevel.level_number * 1000;
        this.score += bonus;
        this.gameState.outcome = 'level-complete';
        this.gameState.next_level = this.currentLevel.level_number + 1;
      }
    
      checkGameOver() {
        return this.lives === 0;
      }
    
      endGame() {
        this.state = 'game-over';
      }
    
      loseBall(ball) {
        const idx = this.balls.indexOf(ball);
        if (idx !== -1) {
          this.balls.splice(idx, 1);
        }
    
        if (this.balls.length === 0) {
          this.lives--;
          if (this.lives <= 0) {
            this.endGame();
          } else {
            this.state = 'life-lost';
            this.powerUp = null;
            this.powerSeconds = 0;
            this.drops = [];
            setTimeout(() => {
              if (this.state === 'life-lost') {
                this.balls = [{
                  x: this.paddle.x,
                  y: this.paddle.y - 12,
                  vx: 0,
                  vy: 0,
                  stuck: true,
                  primary: true
                }];
                this.state = 'ready';
              }
            }, 1000);
          }
        }
      }
    
      updateDrops(dt) {
        for (let i = this.drops.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
          const drop = this.drops[i];
          drop.y += 200 * dt;
    
          const paddleTop = this.paddle.y - this.paddle.height / 2;
          const paddleBottom = this.paddle.y + this.paddle.height / 2;
          const paddleLeft = this.paddle.x - this.paddle.width / 2;
          const paddleRight = this.paddle.x + this.paddle.width / 2;
    
          if (
            drop.x >= paddleLeft &&
            drop.x <= paddleRight &&
            drop.y >= paddleTop &&
            drop.y <= paddleBottom
          ) {
            this.collectPowerUp(drop.type);
            this.drops.splice(i, 1);
          } else if (drop.y > this.canvasHeight) {
            this.drops.splice(i, 1);
          }
        }
      }
    
      spawnDrop(x, y, type) {
        this.drops.push({
          x,
          y,
          type,
          vx: 0,
          vy: 0
        });
      }
    
      collectPowerUp(type) {
        if (this.powerUp && this.powerUp !== type) {
          this.removePowerUp();
        }
    
        this.powerUp = type;
        this.powerSeconds = 20;
    
        if (type === 'wide') {
          this.paddle.width = this.paddle.baseWidth * 1.5;
        } else if (type === 'slow') {
          for (const ball of this.balls) {
            ball.vx *= 0.7;
            ball.vy *= 0.7;
          }
        } else if (type === 'multiball') {
          if (this.balls.length === 1) {
            const primaryBall = this.balls[0];
            this.balls.push({
              x: primaryBall.x - 20,
              y: primaryBall.y,
              vx: primaryBall.vx - 100,
              vy: primaryBall.vy,
              stuck: false,
              primary: false
            });
          }
        } else if (type === 'sticky') {
          // No immediate action
        }
      }
    
      removePowerUp() {
        if (this.powerUp === 'wide') {
          this.paddle.width = this.paddle.baseWidth;
        } else if (this.powerUp === 'slow') {
          for (const ball of this.balls) {
            const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
            const newSpeed = Math.min(speed / 0.7, this.currentLevel?.speed_cap 
|| 520);
            if (speed > 0) {
              ball.vx = (ball.vx / speed) * newSpeed;
              ball.vy = (ball.vy / speed) * newSpeed;
            }
          }
        } else if (this.powerUp === 'multiball') {
          this.balls = this.balls.filter(b => b.primary !== false || 
!b.secondary_only);
        }
      }
    
      updatePowerUp(dt) {
        if (this.powerUp && this.powerSeconds > 0) {
          this.powerSeconds -= dt;
          if (this.powerSeconds <= 0) {
            this.removePowerUp();
            this.powerUp = null;
            this.powerSeconds = 0;
          }
        }
      }
    
      render() {
        this.ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0f1e';
        this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);
    
        this.renderBricks();
        this.renderDrops();
        this.renderPaddle();
        this.renderBalls();
        this.renderUI();
      }
    
      renderBricks() {
        const gridSize = 30;
        const colors = {
          normal: '#56c7ff',
          strong: '#ffd166',
          solid: '#6b6b7e'
        };
    
        for (const key in this.bricks) {
          const brick = this.bricks[key];
          const [row, col] = key.split(',').map(Number);
          const x = col * gridSize + 1;
          const y = row * gridSize + 1;
    
          if (brick.health <= 0 && brick.type !== 'solid') continue;
    
          this.ctx.fillStyle = colors[brick.type] || '#56c7ff';
          this.ctx.fillRect(x, y, gridSize - 2, gridSize - 2);
    
          if (brick.type === 'strong' && brick.health > 0) {
            this.ctx.strokeStyle = brick.health === 1 ? '#ff6b6b' : '#ffd166';
            this.ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            this.ctx.strokeRect(x, y, gridSize - 2, gridSize - 2);
          }
        }
      }
    
      renderDrops() {
        for (const drop of this.drops) {
          const colors = {
            wide: '#56c7ff',
            slow: '#7ee787',
            multiball: '#ffd166',
            sticky: '#ff6b6b'
          };
          
          this.ctx.fillStyle = colors[drop.type] || '#56c7ff';
          this.ctx.beginPath();
          this.ctx.arc(drop.x, drop.y, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          this.ctx.fill();
        }
      }
    
      renderPaddle() {
        this.ctx.fillStyle = '#7ee787';
        this.ctx.fillRect(
          this.paddle.x - this.paddle.width / 2,
          this.paddle.y - this.paddle.height / 2,
          this.paddle.width,
          this.paddle.height
        );
      }
    
      renderBalls() {
        for (const ball of this.balls) {
          this.ctx.fillStyle = ball.primary ? '#56c7ff' : '#ffd166';
          this.ctx.beginPath();
          this.ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          this.ctx.fill();
        }
      }
    
      renderUI() {
        if (this.state === 'ready') {
          this.ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)';
          this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);
          
          this.ctx.fillStyle = '#56c7ff';
          this.ctx.font = 'bold 24px sans-serif';
          this.ctx.textAlign = 'center';
          this.ctx.fillText('Press Space to Launch', this.canvasWidth / 2, 
this.canvasHeight / 2);
        } else if (this.state === 'paused') {
          this.ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6)';
          this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);
          
          this.ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd166';
          this.ctx.font = 'bold 24px sans-serif';
          this.ctx.textAlign = 'center';
          this.ctx.fillText('PAUSED', this.canvasWidth / 2, this.canvasHeight / 
2);
        } else if (this.state === 'life-lost') {
          this.ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)';
          this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);
          
          this.ctx.fillStyle = '#ff6b6b';
          this.ctx.font = 'bold 24px sans-serif';
          this.ctx.textAlign = 'center';
          this.ctx.fillText('LIFE LOST', this.canvasWidth / 2, this.canvasHeight
/ 2);
        }
      }
    
      async saveState() {
        if (!this.gameState || !this.gameState.run_id) return;
    
        try {
          const stateToSave = {
            level: this.currentLevel.level_number,
            score: this.score,
            lives: this.lives,
            combo: this.combo,
            next_extra_life: this.nextExtraLife,
            power_up: this.powerUp,
            power_seconds: this.powerSeconds,
            paddle_width: this.paddle.width,
            balls: this.balls,
            drops: this.drops,
            bricks: this.bricks,
            accumulated_time: this.accumulator
          };
    
          await window.api.saveRun(
            this.gameState.run_id,
            this.gameState.revision,
            stateToSave
          );
    
          this.gameState.revision++;
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Error saving state:', err);
        }
      }
    
      destroy() {
        if (this.animationFrameId) {
          cancelAnimationFrame(this.animationFrameId);
        }
      }
    }
    
    window.game = new BrickfallGame();

+93|      this.assistPaddle = false;
+94|    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd') {
+95|      this.paddle.vx = this.paddle.speed;
+96|      this.assistPaddle = false;
+97|    } else if (e.key === ' ') {
+98|      e.preventDefault();
+99|      this.handleLaunch();
+100|    } else if (e.key === 'p' || e.key === 'P' || e.key === 'Escape') {
+101|      e.preventDefault();
+102|      this.togglePause();
+103|    } else if (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R') {
+104|      e.preventDefault();
+105|      if (this.state === 'game-over') {
+106|        this.handleRestart();
+107|      }
+108|    }
+109|  }
+110|
+111|  handleKeyUp(e) {
+112|    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'ArrowRight' ||
e.key === 'd') {
+113|      this.paddle.vx = 0;
+114|    }
+115|  }
+116|
+117|  handleLaunch() {
+118|    if (this.state === 'ready' || (this.state === 'playing' && 
this.balls.some(b => b.stuck))) {
+119|      const stuckBall = this.balls.find(b => b.stuck);
+120|      if (stuckBall) {
+121|        stuckBall.stuck = false;
+122|        stuckBall.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
+123|        stuckBall.vy = -300;
+124|      }
+125|    }
+126|  }
+127|
+128|  togglePause() {
+129|    if (this.state === 'playing') {
+130|      this.state = 'paused';
+131|      this.isPaused = true;
+132|    } else if (this.state === 'paused') {
+133|      this.state = 'playing';
+134|      this.isPaused = false;
+135|      this.accumulator = 0;
+136|    }
+137|  }
+138|
+139|  handleRestart() {
+140|    if (this.state === 'game-over' && this.gameState) {
+141|      window.ui.startNewRun(this.currentLevel.level_number || 
this.currentLevel.level);
+142|    }
+143|  }
+144|
+145|  async loadLevel(levelNumber) {
+146|    const gameState = await window.api.getGameState();
+147|    
+148|    const levels = gameState.user;
+149|    const levelData = {
+150|      level_number: levelNumber,
+151|      base_speed: gameState.activeRun ? gameState.activeRun.level : 
levelNumber,
+152|      speed_cap: 520 + (levelNumber - 1) * 25
+153|    };
+154|
+155|    this.currentLevel = levelData;
+156|    this.currentBrickLayout = gameState.activeRun?.bricks || {};
+157|    
+158|    this.gameState = gameState.activeRun || {
+159|      run_id: null,
+160|      revision: 0,
+161|      level: levelNumber,
+162|      score: 0,
+163|      lives: 3,
+164|      combo: 1,
+165|      next_extra_life: 20000,
+166|      power_up: null,
+167|      power_seconds: 0,
+168|      paddle_width: 118,
+169|      balls: [{ x: 189, y: 540, vx: 0, vy: 0, stuck: true, primary: true 
}],
+170|      drops: [],
+171|      bricks: this.currentBrickLayout,
+172|      accumulated_time: 0
+173|    };
+174|
+175|    this.score = this.gameState.score;
+176|    this.lives = this.gameState.lives;
+177|    this.combo = this.gameState.combo;
+178|    this.nextExtraLife = this.gameState.next_extra_life;
+179|    this.powerUp = this.gameState.power_up;
+180|    this.powerSeconds = this.gameState.power_seconds || 0;
+181|    this.paddle.width = this.gameState.paddle_width;
+182|    this.balls = this.gameState.balls.map(b => ({ ...b }));
+183|    this.drops = this.gameState.drops.map(d => ({ ...d }));
+184|    this.bricks = this.gameState.bricks ? 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.gameState.bricks)) : {};
+185|    
+186|    this.accumulator = this.gameState.accumulated_time || 0;
+187|    
+188|    if (!this.gameState.run_id) {
+189|      const response = await window.api.startRun(levelNumber);
+190|      this.gameState = response;
+191|      this.gameState.balls = this.balls;
+192|      this.gameState.drops = this.drops;
+193|      this.gameState.bricks = this.bricks;
+194|    }
+195|
+196|    this.state = 'ready';
+197|    this.startGameLoop();
+198|  }
+199|
+200|  startGameLoop() {
+201|    const animate = (time) => {
+202|      if (!this.lastFrameTime) {
+203|        this.lastFrameTime = time;
+204|      }
+205|
+206|      const deltaTime = Math.min((time - this.lastFrameTime) / 1000, 
0.033);
+207|      this.lastFrameTime = time;
+208|
+209|      if (!this.isPaused && this.state === 'playing') {
+210|        this.accumulator += deltaTime;
+211|        this.accumulator = Math.min(this.accumulator, 
this.MAX_ACCUMULATOR);
+212|
+213|        while (this.accumulator >= this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP) {
+214|          this.update(this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP);
+215|          this.accumulator -= this.PHYSICS_TIMESTEP;
+216|        }
+217|      }
+218|
+219|      this.render();
+220|      this.animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
+221|    };
+222|
+223|    this.animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
+224|  }
+225|
+226|  update(dt) {
+227|    if (this.state !== 'playing') return;
+228|
+229|    this.updatePaddle(dt);
+230|    this.updateBalls(dt);
+231|    this.updateDrops(dt);
+232|    this.updatePowerUp(dt);
+233|
+234|    const allBroken = this.checkLevelComplete();
+235|    if (allBroken) {
+236|      this.completeLevel();
+237|    }
+238|
+239|    const noLives = this.checkGameOver();
+240|    if (noLives) {
+241|      this.endGame();
+242|    }
+243|  }
+244|
+245|  updatePaddle(dt) {
+246|    this.paddle.x += this.paddle.vx * dt;
+247|    this.paddle.x = Math.max(
+248|      this.paddle.width / 2,
+249|      Math.min(this.canvasWidth - this.paddle.width / 2, this.paddle.x)
+250|    );
+251|    this.lastPaddleX = this.paddle.x;
+252|  }
+253|
+254|  updateBalls(dt) {
+255|    for (const ball of this.balls) {
+256|      if (ball.stuck) {
+257|        ball.x = this.paddle.x;
+258|        ball.y = this.paddle.y - 12;
+259|        continue;
+260|      }
+261|
+262|      const startX = ball.x;
+263|      const startY = ball.y;
+264|
+265|      ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
+266|      ball.y += ball.vy * dt;
+267|
+268|      this.ensureMinimumSpeed(ball);
+269|      this.capBallSpeed(ball);
+270|
+271|      this.checkWallCollisions(ball, startX, startY, dt);
+272|      this.checkPaddleCollision(ball, startX, startY);
+273|      this.checkBrickCollisions(ball, startX, startY);
+274|    }
+275|  }
+276|
+277|  ensureMinimumSpeed(ball) {
+278|    const minSpeed = 100;
+279|    const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
+280|    if (speed < minSpeed && speed > 0) {
+281|      const scale = minSpeed / speed;
+282|      ball.vx *= scale;
+283|      ball.vy *= scale;
+284|    }
+285|  }
+286|
+287|  capBallSpeed(ball) {
+288|    const levelSpeed = this.currentLevel?.speed_cap || 520;
+289|    const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
+290|    if (speed > levelSpeed) {
+291|      const scale = levelSpeed / speed;
+292|      ball.vx *= scale;
+293|      ball.vy *= scale;
+294|    }
+295|  }
+296|
+297|  checkWallCollisions(ball, startX, startY, dt) {
+298|    const radius = 6;
+299|
+300|    if (ball.x - radius < 0) {
+301|      ball.x = radius;
+302|      ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx);
+303|    } else if (ball.x + radius > this.canvasWidth) {
+304|      ball.x = this.canvasWidth - radius;
+305|      ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx);
+306|    }
+307|
+308|    if (ball.y - radius < 0) {
+309|      ball.y = radius;
+310|      ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy);
+311|    } else if (ball.y > this.canvasHeight) {
+312|      this.loseBall(ball);
+313|    }
+314|  }
+315|
+316|  checkPaddleCollision(ball, startX, startY) {
+317|    const paddleTop = this.paddle.y - this.paddle.height / 2;
+318|    const paddleBottom = this.paddle.y + this.paddle.height / 2;
+319|    const paddleLeft = this.paddle.x - this.paddle.width / 2;
+320|    const paddleRight = this.paddle.x + this.paddle.width / 2;
+321|    const ballRadius = 6;
+322|
+323|    if (
+324|      ball.x + ballRadius >= paddleLeft &&
+325|      ball.x - ballRadius <= paddleRight &&
+326|      ball.y + ballRadius >= paddleTop &&
+327|      ball.y - ballRadius <= paddleBottom &&
+328|      startY < paddleTop
+329|    ) {
+330|      ball.y = paddleTop - ballRadius;
+331|      
+332|      const contactPos = (ball.x - paddleLeft) / this.paddle.width;
+333|      const angle = (contactPos - 0.5) * Math.PI * 0.4;
+334|      const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) || 300;
+335|      
+336|      ball.vx = Math.sin(angle) * speed * 0.8 + this.paddle.vx * 0.3;
+337|      ball.vy = -Math.cos(angle) * speed;
+338|      
+339|      this.combo = 1;
+340|      
+341|      if (this.powerUp === 'sticky' && !ball.stuck) {
+342|        ball.stuck = true;
+343|        this.powerUp = null;
+344|        this.powerSeconds = 0;
+345|      }
+346|    }
+347|  }
+348|
+349|  checkBrickCollisions(ball, startX, startY) {
+350|    const ballRadius = 6;
+351|    const gridSize = 30;
+352|
+353|    for (const key in this.bricks) {
+354|      const brick = this.bricks[key];
+355|      if (brick.health <= 0) continue;
+356|
+357|      const [row, col] = key.split(',').map(Number);
+358|      const brickX = col * gridSize + gridSize / 2;
+359|      const brickY = row * gridSize + gridSize / 2;
+360|      const brickW = gridSize - 2;
+361|      const brickH = gridSize - 2;
+362|
+363|      if (this.sweptCollision(startX, startY, ball.x, ball.y, ballRadius, 
brickX, brickY, brickW, brickH)) {
+364|        const damage = this.damageBrick(key, brick);
+365|        if (damage > 0) {
+366|          this.addScore(damage);
+367|          
+368|          const closestPoint = this.closestPointOnRect(ball.x, ball.y, 
brickX - brickW/2, brickY - brickH/2, brickW, brickH);
+369|          const dx = ball.x - closestPoint.x;
+370|          const dy = ball.y - closestPoint.y;
+371|          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
+372|          
+373|          if (dist > 0) {
+374|            ball.vx = (dx / dist) * Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 0.8;
+375|            ball.vy = (dy / dist) * Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 0.8;
+376|          }
+377|          
+378|          if (brick.drop && Math.random() < 0.3) {
+379|            this.spawnDrop(brickX, brickY, brick.drop);
+380|          }
+381|        }
+382|        break;
+383|      }
+384|    }
+385|  }
+386|
+387|  sweptCollision(x1, y1, x2, y2, r, bx, by, bw, bh) {
+388|    const left = bx - bw / 2;
+389|    const right = bx + bw / 2;
+390|    const top = by - bh / 2;
+391|    const bottom = by + bh / 2;
+392|
+393|    const dx = x2 - x1;
+394|    const dy = y2 - y1;
+395|    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
+396|
+397|    if (dist === 0) {
+398|      return x2 > left && x2 < right && y2 > top && y2 < bottom;
+399|    }
+400|
+401|    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((bx - x1) * dx + (by - y1) * dy) / 
(dist * dist)));
+402|    const closestX = x1 + t * dx;
+403|    const closestY = y1 + t * dy;
+404|
+405|    const distToBrick = Math.hypot(bx - closestX, by - closestY);
+406|    return distToBrick <= r;
+407|  }
+408|
+409|  closestPointOnRect(px, py, left, top, width, height) {
+410|    return {
+411|      x: Math.max(left, Math.min(px, left + width)),
+412|      y: Math.max(top, Math.min(py, top + height))
+413|    };
+414|  }
+415|
+416|  damageBrick(key, brick) {
+417|    if (brick.type === 'solid') {
+418|      return 0;
+419|    }
+420|    
+421|    if (brick.health <= 0) {
+422|      return 0;
+423|    }
+424|
+425|    brick.health--;
+426|    
+427|    if (brick.type === 'normal') {
+428|      if (brick.health === 0) {
+429|        return 100 * this.combo;
+430|      }
+431|    } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
+432|      if (brick.health === 1) {
+433|        return 75 * this.combo;
+434|      } else if (brick.health === 0) {
+435|        return 250 * this.combo;
+436|      }
+437|    }
+438|    
+439|    return 0;
+440|  }
+441|
+442|  addScore(points) {
+443|    const oldScore = this.score;
+444|    this.score += points;
+445|    
+446|    const oldThreshold = Math.floor(oldScore / 20000);
+447|    const newThreshold = Math.floor(this.score / 20000);
+448|    
+449|    if (newThreshold > oldThreshold) {
+450|      this.lives++;
+451|      this.nextExtraLife = (newThreshold + 1) * 20000;
+452|    }
+453|    
+454|    this.combo = Math.min(5, this.combo + 1);
+455|  }
+456|
+457|  checkLevelComplete() {
+458|    return Object.values(this.bricks).every(b => b.type === 'solid' || 
b.health <= 0);
+459|  }
+460|
+461|  completeLevel() {
+462|    this.state = 'level-complete';
+463|    const bonus = this.currentLevel.level_number * 1000;
+464|    this.score += bonus;
+465|    this.gameState.outcome = 'level-complete';
+466|    this.gameState.next_level = this.currentLevel.level_number + 1;
+467|  }
+468|
+469|  checkGameOver() {
+470|    return this.lives === 0;
+471|  }
+472|
+473|  endGame() {
+474|    this.state = 'game-over';
+475|  }
+476|
+477|  loseBall(ball) {
+478|    const idx = this.balls.indexOf(ball);
+479|    if (idx !== -1) {
+480|      this.balls.splice(idx, 1);
+481|    }
+482|
+483|    if (this.balls.length === 0) {
+484|      this.lives--;
+485|      if (this.lives <= 0) {
+486|        this.endGame();
+487|      } else {
+488|        this.state = 'life-lost';
+489|        this.powerUp = null;
+490|        this.powerSeconds = 0;
+491|        this.drops = [];
+492|        setTimeout(() => {
+493|          if (this.state === 'life-lost') {
+494|            this.balls = [{
+495|              x: this.paddle.x,
+496|              y: this.paddle.y - 12,
+497|              vx: 0,
+498|              vy: 0,
+499|              stuck: true,
+500|              primary: true
+501|            }];
+502|            this.state = 'ready';
+503|          }
+504|        }, 1000);
+505|      }
+506|    }
+507|  }
+508|
+509|  updateDrops(dt) {
+510|    for (let i = this.drops.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+511|      const drop = this.drops[i];
+512|      drop.y += 200 * dt;
+513|
+514|      const paddleTop = this.paddle.y - this.paddle.height / 2;
+515|      const paddleBottom = this.paddle.y + this.paddle.height / 2;
+516|      const paddleLeft = this.paddle.x - this.paddle.width / 2;
+517|      const paddleRight = this.paddle.x + this.paddle.width / 2;
+518|
+519|      if (
+520|        drop.x >= paddleLeft &&
+521|        drop.x <= paddleRight &&
+522|        drop.y >= paddleTop &&
+523|        drop.y <= paddleBottom
+524|      ) {
+525|        this.collectPowerUp(drop.type);
+526|        this.drops.splice(i, 1);
+527|      } else if (drop.y > this.canvasHeight) {
+528|        this.drops.splice(i, 1);
+529|      }
+530|    }
+531|  }
+532|
+533|  spawnDrop(x, y, type) {
+534|    this.drops.push({
+535|      x,
+536|      y,
+537|      type,
+538|      vx: 0,
+539|      vy: 0
+540|    });
+541|  }
+542|
+543|  collectPowerUp(type) {
+544|    if (this.powerUp && this.powerUp !== type) {
+545|      this.removePowerUp();
+546|    }
+547|
+548|    this.powerUp = type;
+549|    this.powerSeconds = 20;
+550|
+551|    if (type === 'wide') {
+552|      this.paddle.width = this.paddle.baseWidth * 1.5;
+553|    } else if (type === 'slow') {
+554|      for (const ball of this.balls) {
+555|        ball.vx *= 0.7;
+556|        ball.vy *= 0.7;
+557|      }
+558|    } else if (type === 'multiball') {
+559|      if (this.balls.length === 1) {
+560|        const primaryBall = this.balls[0];
+561|        this.balls.push({
+562|          x: primaryBall.x - 20,
+563|          y: primaryBall.y,
+564|          vx: primaryBall.vx - 100,
+565|          vy: primaryBall.vy,
+566|          stuck: false,
+567|          primary: false
+568|        });
+569|      }
+570|    } else if (type === 'sticky') {
+571|      // No immediate action
+572|    }
+573|  }
+574|
+575|  removePowerUp() {
+576|    if (this.powerUp === 'wide') {
+577|      this.paddle.width = this.paddle.baseWidth;
+578|    } else if (this.powerUp === 'slow') {
+579|      for (const ball of this.balls) {
+580|        const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
+581|        const newSpeed = Math.min(speed / 0.7, this.currentLevel?.speed_cap
|| 520);
+582|        if (speed > 0) {
+583|          ball.vx = (ball.vx / speed) * newSpeed;
+584|          ball.vy = (ball.vy / speed) * newSpeed;
+585|        }
+586|      }
+587|    } else if (this.powerUp === 'multiball') {
+588|      this.balls = this.balls.filter(b => b.primary !== false || 
!b.secondary_only);
+589|    }
+590|  }
+591|
+592|  updatePowerUp(dt) {
+593|    if (this.powerUp && this.powerSeconds > 0) {
+594|      this.powerSeconds -= dt;
+595|      if (this.powerSeconds <= 0) {
+596|        this.removePowerUp();
+597|        this.powerUp = null;
+598|        this.powerSeconds = 0;
+599|      }
+600|    }
+601|  }
+602|
+603|  render() {
+604|    this.ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0f1e';
+605|    this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);
+606|
+607|    this.renderBricks();
+608|    this.renderDrops();
+609|    this.renderPaddle();
+610|    this.renderBalls();
+611|    this.renderUI();
+612|  }
+613|
+614|  renderBricks() {
+615|    const gridSize = 30;
+616|    const colors = {
+617|      normal: '#56c7ff',
+618|      strong: '#ffd166',
+619|      solid: '#6b6b7e'
+620|    };
+621|
+622|    for (const key in this.bricks) {
+623|      const brick = this.bricks[key];
+624|      const [row, col] = key.split(',').map(Number);
+625|      const x = col * gridSize + 1;
+626|      const y = row * gridSize + 1;
+627|
+628|      if (brick.health <= 0 && brick.type !== 'solid') continue;
+629|
+630|      this.ctx.fillStyle = colors[brick.type] || '#56c7ff';
+631|      this.ctx.fillRect(x, y, gridSize - 2, gridSize - 2);
+632|
+633|      if (brick.type === 'strong' && brick.health > 0) {
+634|        this.ctx.strokeStyle = brick.health === 1 ? '#ff6b6b' : '#ffd166';
+635|        this.ctx.lineWidth = 2;
+636|        this.ctx.strokeRect(x, y, gridSize - 2, gridSize - 2);
+637|      }
+638|    }
+639|  }
+640|
+641|  renderDrops() {
+642|    for (const drop of this.drops) {
+643|      const colors = {
+644|        wide: '#56c7ff',
+645|        slow: '#7ee787',
+646|        multiball: '#ffd166',
+647|        sticky: '#ff6b6b'
+648|      };
+649|      
+650|      this.ctx.fillStyle = colors[drop.type] || '#56c7ff';
+651|      this.ctx.beginPath();
+652|      this.ctx.arc(drop.x, drop.y, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
+653|      this.ctx.fill();
+654|    }
+655|  }
+656|
+657|  renderPaddle() {
+658|    this.ctx.fillStyle = '#7ee787';
+659|    this.ctx.fillRect(
+660|      this.paddle.x - this.paddle.width / 2,
+661|      this.paddle.y - this.paddle.height / 2,
+662|      this.paddle.width,
+663|      this.paddle.height
+664|    );
+665|  }
+666|
+667|  renderBalls() {
+668|    for (const ball of this.balls) {
+669|      this.ctx.fillStyle = ball.primary ? '#56c7ff' : '#ffd166';
+670|      this.ctx.beginPath();
+671|      this.ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
+672|      this.ctx.fill();
+673|    }
+674|  }
+675|
+676|  renderUI() {
+677|    if (this.state === 'ready') {
+678|      this.ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)';
+679|      this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);
+680|      
+681|      this.ctx.fillStyle = '#56c7ff';
+682|      this.ctx.font = 'bold 24px sans-serif';
+683|      this.ctx.textAlign = 'center';
+684|      this.ctx.fillText('Press Space to Launch', this.canvasWidth / 2, 
this.canvasHeight / 2);
+685|    } else if (this.state === 'paused') {
+686|      this.ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6)';

      constructor() {
        this.currentUser = null;
        this.gameState = null;
        this.lastSaveTime = 0;
        this.saveInterval = 5000;

      }

        document.getElementById('sign-in-form').addEventListener('submit', (e)

      }
    
      async handleSignIn(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        
        const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
        const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
        const messageEl = document.getElementById('sign-in-message');
    
        try {
          messageEl.textContent = 'Signing in...';
          const result = await window.api.signIn(email, password);
          
          window.api.setToken(result.token);
          this.currentUser = result.user;
    
          this.showGameScreen();
          await this.loadGameState();
        } catch (err) {
          messageEl.textContent = err.data?.error || 'Sign in failed';
          messageEl.className = 'message error';
        }
      }
    
      async handleSignOut() {
        try {
          await window.api.signOut();
          window.api.clearToken();
          this.currentUser = null;
          window.game.destroy();
          this.showSignInScreen();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Sign out error:', err);
        }
      }
    
      showSignInScreen() {
        document.getElementById('sign-in-screen').classList.add('active');

      }

        document.getElementById('player-name').textContent = `${playerName}

      }

this.gameState.user.best_score;
    
          this.renderLevelButtons();
          this.renderLeaderboard(this.gameState.leaderboard);
          this.renderRunHistory(this.gameState.runHistory);
    
          if (this.gameState.activeRun) {
            this.showContinueOption();
            window.game.loadLevel(this.gameState.activeRun.level);
          }
    
          this.startAutoSave();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Error loading game state:', err);
        }
      }
    
      renderLevelButtons() {
        const container = document.getElementById('level-buttons');
        container.innerHTML = '';
    
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
          const btn = document.createElement('button');
          btn.className = 'level-btn';
          btn.textContent = i;
    
          if (i <= this.gameState.unlockedLevels) {
            btn.className += ' unlocked';
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => this.startNewRun(i));
          } else {
            btn.disabled = true;
          }
    
          if (this.gameState.activeRun && this.gameState.activeRun.level === i) 
{
            btn.className += ' active';
          }
    
          container.appendChild(btn);
        }
      }
    
      renderLeaderboard(entries) {
        const container = document.getElementById('leaderboard');
        container.innerHTML = '';
    
        entries.forEach((entry, idx) => {
          const div = document.createElement('div');
          div.className = 'leaderboard-entry';
          
          div.innerHTML = `
            <span class="leaderboard-rank">#${idx + 1}</span>
            <span class="leaderboard-name">${entry.initials}</span>
            <span class="leaderboard-score">${entry.score}</span>
          `;
          
          container.appendChild(div);
        });
      }
    
      renderRunHistory(history) {
        const container = document.getElementById('run-history');
        container.innerHTML = '';
    
        if (!history || history.length === 0) {

      async startNewRun(levelNumber) {
        try {

          document.getElementById('continue-btn').style.display =

+95|  renderLevelButtons() {

+103|
+104|      if (i <= this.gameState.unlockedLevels) {

+113|      }
+114|
+115|      container.appendChild(btn);
+116|    }

+137|  renderRunHistory(history) {

'game-over';

+152|
+153|      div.innerHTML = `
+154|        <div class="history-entry-header">
+155|          <span>Level ${record.level}</span>
+156|          <span class="history-outcome 
${outcomeClass}">${record.outcome}</span>
+157|        </div>
+158|        <div class="history-details">
+159|          <div class="history-detail-item">
+160|            <span class="history-detail-label">Score:</span>
+161|            <span class="history-detail-value">${record.score}</span>
+162|          </div>
+163|          <div class="history-detail-item">
+164|            <span class="history-detail-label">Date:</span>
+165|            <span class="history-detail-value">${dateStr}</span>
+166|          </div>
+167|        </div>
+168|      `;

+172|  }

  new_str: 
      async loadLevel(levelNumber) {
        const gameState = await window.api.getGameState();
        
        // Find level info from the game state
        let levelData = null;
        if (gameState.levels) {
          levelData = gameState.levels.find(l => l.level_number === 
levelNumber);
        }
        
        if (!levelData) {
          // Fallback to calculated values
          levelData = {
            level_number: levelNumber,
            name: `Level ${levelNumber}`,
            base_speed: 300 + (levelNumber - 1) * 18,
            speed_cap: 520 + (levelNumber - 1) * 25,
            accent: '#56c7ff'
          };
        }
    
        this.currentLevel = levelData;
        this.currentBrickLayout = gameState.activeRun?.bricks || {};
        
        this.gameState = gameState.activeRun || {
          run_id: null,
          revision: 0,
          level: levelNumber,
          score: 0,
          lives: 3,
          combo: 1,
          next_extra_life: 20000,
          power_up: null,
          power_seconds: 0,
          paddle_width: 118,
          balls: [{ x: 189, y: 540, vx: 0, vy: 0, stuck: true, primary: true }],
          drops: [],
          bricks: this.currentBrickLayout,
          accumulated_time: 0
        };
    
        this.score = this.gameState.score;
        this.lives = this.gameState.lives;
        this.combo = this.gameState.combo;
        this.nextExtraLife = this.gameState.next_extra_life;
        this.powerUp = this.gameState.power_up;
        this.powerSeconds = this.gameState.power_seconds || 0;
        this.paddle.width = this.gameState.paddle_width;
        this.balls = (this.gameState.balls || []).map(b => ({ ...b }));
        this.drops = (this.gameState.drops || []).map(d => ({ ...d }));
        this.bricks = this.gameState.bricks ? 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.gameState.bricks)) : {};
        
        this.accumulator = this.gameState.accumulated_time || 0;
        
        if (!this.gameState.run_id) {
          const response = await window.api.startRun(levelNumber);
          this.gameState = response;
          this.balls = response.balls || this.balls;
          this.drops = response.drops || this.drops;
          this.bricks = response.bricks || this.bricks;
        }
    
        this.state = 'ready';
        this.startGameLoop();
      }

    // Get game state
    app.get('/api/game-state', authMiddleware, (req, res) => {
      const db = getDB();
      
      const user = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = 
?').get(req.user.id);
      const activeRun = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM active_runs WHERE user_id = 
?').get(req.user.id);
      
      const leaderboard = db.prepare(`
        SELECT initials, score, level, achieved_at 
        FROM leaderboard 
        ORDER BY score DESC, achieved_at ASC
        LIMIT 10
      `).all();
      
      const unlockedLevels = user.highest_level || 1;
      
      const runHistory = db.prepare(`
        SELECT run_id, outcome, level, score, finished_at
        FROM run_records
        WHERE user_id = ?
        ORDER BY finished_at DESC
        LIMIT 10
      `).all(req.user.id);
      
      db.close();
      
      res.json({
        user: {
          id: user.id,
          email: user.email,
          name: user.name,
          initials: user.initials,
          best_score: user.best_score
        },

      async saveState() {
        if (!this.gameState || !this.gameState.run_id) return;
    
        try {
          const stateToSave = {
            level: this.currentLevel.level_number,
            score: this.score,
            lives: this.lives,
            combo: this.combo,
            next_extra_life: this.nextExtraLife,
            power_up: this.powerUp,
            power_seconds: this.powerSeconds,
            paddle_width: this.paddle.width,
            balls: this.balls,
            drops: this.drops,
            bricks: this.bricks,
            accumulated_time: this.accumulator
          };
    
          await window.api.saveRun(
            this.gameState.run_id,
            this.gameState.revision,
            stateToSave
          );
    
          this.gameState.revision++;
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Error saving state:', err);
        }
      }
    
      async finishRun(outcome) {
        if (!this.gameState || !this.gameState.run_id) return;
    
        try {
          const result = await window.api.finishRun(
            this.gameState.run_id,
            this.gameState.revision,
            outcome,
            this.currentLevel.level_number,
            this.score
          );
    
          window.ui.gameState.leaderboard = result.leaderboard;
          window.ui.renderLeaderboard(result.leaderboard);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Error finishing run:', err);
        }
      }

        if (this.animationFrameId) {

      checkBrickCollisions(ball, startX, startY) {
        const ballRadius = 6;
        const gridSize = 30;
    
        for (const key in this.bricks) {
          const brick = this.bricks[key];
          if (brick.health <= 0) continue;
    
          const [row, col] = key.split(',').map(Number);
          const brickX = col * gridSize + gridSize / 2;
          const brickY = row * gridSize + gridSize / 2;
          const brickW = gridSize - 2;
          const brickH = gridSize - 2;
    
          if (this.sweptCollision(startX, startY, ball.x, ball.y, ballRadius, 
brickX, brickY, brickW, brickH)) {
            const damage = this.damageBrick(key, brick);
            if (damage > 0) {
              this.addScore(damage);
              
              const closestPoint = this.closestPointOnRect(ball.x, ball.y, 
brickX - brickW/2, brickY - brickH/2, brickW, brickH);
              const dx = ball.x - closestPoint.x;
              const dy = ball.y - closestPoint.y;
              const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
              
              if (dist > 0) {
                ball.vx = (dx / dist) * Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 0.8;
                ball.vy = (dy / dist) * Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 0.8;
              }
              
              if (brick.drop && Math.random() < 0.3) {
                this.spawnDrop(brickX, brickY, brick.drop);
              }
            }
            break;
          }
        }
      }

  new_str: 
      checkBrickCollisions(ball, startX, startY) {
        const ballRadius = 6;
        const gridSize = 30;
        let closestDist = Infinity;
        let closestBrick = null;
    
        // Find closest collision
        for (const key in this.bricks) {
          const brick = this.bricks[key];
          if (brick.health <= 0) continue;
    
          const [row, col] = key.split(',').map(Number);
          const brickX = col * gridSize + gridSize / 2;
          const brickY = row * gridSize + gridSize / 2;
          const brickW = gridSize - 2;
          const brickH = gridSize - 2;
    
          if (this.sweptCollision(startX, startY, ball.x, ball.y, ballRadius, 
brickX, brickY, brickW, brickH)) {
            const dist = Math.hypot(brickX - startX, brickY - startY);
            if (dist < closestDist) {
              closestDist = dist;
              closestBrick = { key, brick, x: brickX, y: brickY, w: brickW, h: 
brickH };
            }
          }
        }
    
        if (closestBrick) {
          const damage = this.damageBrick(closestBrick.key, closestBrick.brick);
          if (damage > 0) {
            this.addScore(damage);
            
            // Reflect ball based on brick surface
            const left = closestBrick.x - closestBrick.w/2;
            const right = closestBrick.x + closestBrick.w/2;
            const top = closestBrick.y - closestBrick.h/2;
            const bottom = closestBrick.y + closestBrick.h/2;
            
            // Determine which edge was hit
            const overlapLeft = Math.abs(ball.x - left);
            const overlapRight = Math.abs(ball.x - right);
            const overlapTop = Math.abs(ball.y - top);
            const overlapBottom = Math.abs(ball.y - bottom);
            
            const minOverlap = Math.min(overlapLeft, overlapRight, overlapTop, 
overlapBottom);
            
            if (minOverlap === overlapLeft || minOverlap === overlapRight) {
              // Vertical collision
              ball.vx = -ball.vx;
            } else {
              // Horizontal collision
              ball.vy = -ball.vy;
            }
            
            // Spawn power-up if applicable
            if (closestBrick.brick.drop) {
              this.spawnDrop(closestBrick.x, closestBrick.y, 
closestBrick.brick.drop);
            }
          }
        }
      }

+402|        const right = closestBrick.x + closestBrick.w/2;
+403|        const top = closestBrick.y - closestBrick.h/2;
+404|        const bottom = closestBrick.y + closestBrick.h/2;
+405|        
+406|        // Determine which edge was hit
+407|        const overlapLeft = Math.abs(ball.x - left);
+408|        const overlapRight = Math.abs(ball.x - right);
+409|        const overlapTop = Math.abs(ball.y - top);
+410|        const overlapBottom = Math.abs(ball.y - bottom);
+411|        
+412|        const minOverlap = Math.min(overlapLeft, overlapRight, overlapTop, 
overlapBottom);
+413|        
+414|        if (minOverlap === overlapLeft || minOverlap === overlapRight) {
+415|          // Vertical collision
+416|          ball.vx = -ball.vx;
+417|        } else {
+418|          // Horizontal collision
+419|          ball.vy = -ball.vy;
+420|        }
+421|        
+422|        // Spawn power-up if applicable
+423|        if (closestBrick.brick.drop) {
+424|          this.spawnDrop(closestBrick.x, closestBrick.y, 
closestBrick.brick.drop);
+425|        }
 426|      }
 427|    }
[end of edit 2 / 2]
